dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eIt's a desktop app that finds Business Profiles from Google Maps for any category and location you choose — restaurants, dentists, gyms, agencies, and more. In a few clicks you get a clean, exportable list of businesses along with their contact details, so you can reach out and sell to them.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eFounders use it to find their first customers; students use it to gather data for research projects.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eIf you are a developer, this app has a \\u003cstrong\\u003ebuilt-in API\\u003c/strong\\u003e, an \\u003ca href=\\\"https://www.npmjs.com/package/botasaurus-desktop-api\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003enpm package\\u003c/a\\u003e, and a\\n\\u003ca href=\\\"https://pypi.org/project/botasaurus-api/\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003ePython library\\u003c/a\\u003e, so you can integrate it into your Python or Node.js scripts and get results in under 5 minutes. The API can also run on \\u003ca href=\\\"https://github.com/omkarcloud/google-maps-scraper/blob/master/server-deployment.md\\\"\\u003eAWS and GCP VMs\\u003c/a\\u003e.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch3 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" class=\\\"heading-element\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eWhat data can I get from it?\\u003c/h3\\u003e\\u003ca id=\\\"user-content-what-data-can-i-get-from-it\\\" class=\\\"anchor\\\" aria-label=\\\"Permalink: What data can I get from it?\\\" href=\\\"#what-data-can-i-get-from-it\\\"\\u003e\\u003csvg data-component=\\\"Octicon\\\" class=\\\"octicon octicon-link\\\" viewBox=\\\"0 0 16 16\\\" version=\\\"1.1\\\" width=\\\"16\\\" height=\\\"16\\\" aria-hidden=\\\"true\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpath d=\\\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/path\\u003e\\u003c/svg\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eFor every business, you get the major data points you need to sell:\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eName, Main Category, and Address\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003ePhone number\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eWebsite\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eEmail and social profiles (via enrichment)\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eRating and number of reviews\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eThat's just the start — the tool extracts \\u003cstrong\\u003e50+ data points\\u003c/strong\\u003e in total. See the full list in \\u003ca href=\\\"https://github.com/omkarcloud/google-maps-scraper/blob/master/advanced.md#are-there-any-usage-limits\\\"\\u003eadvanced.md (Are there any usage limits?)\\u003c/a\\u003e.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eOn top of the raw data, \\u003cstrong\\u003ebusiness enrichment\\u003c/strong\\u003e gets you sales-ready contacts: verified emails, decision-makers (CEO, Founder, etc.), and phone carrier info so you don't waste time on landlines.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch3 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" class=\\\"heading-element\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eHow to use it?\\u003c/h3\\u003e\\u003ca id=\\\"user-content-how-to-use-it\\\" class=\\\"anchor\\\" aria-label=\\\"Permalink: How to use it?\\\" href=\\\"#how-to-use-it\\\"\\u003e\\u003csvg data-component=\\\"Octicon\\\" class=\\\"octicon octicon-link\\\" viewBox=\\\"0 0 16 16\\\" version=\\\"1.1\\\" width=\\\"16\\\" height=\\\"16\\\" aria-hidden=\\\"true\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpath d=\\\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/path\\u003e\\u003c/svg\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eTo use this tool, ensure you have \\u003cstrong\\u003eGoogle Chrome\\u003c/strong\\u003e installed. must understand this.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eThere are 5 kinds of search strategies:\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eFast (Default)\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eFastest\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eDetailed\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eBy Zoom Level (15, 16, 17, 18)\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eBy Geolocation\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ca target=\\\"_blank\\\" rel=\\\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\\\" src=\\\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/omkarcloud/google-maps-scraper/master/screenshots/search-strategies.png\\\" alt=\\\"search-strategies.png\\\" style=\\\"max-width: 100%;\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eLet's see when to use which strategy:\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eFast (Default):\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eReturns 120–1600 results per city.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eCompletes in 1–10 minutes per city.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eWhen to use:\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eBest for country-level extraction (completes in 1–2 days).\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eFastest:\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eSlightly faster than Fast mode (~30 seconds per city), but returns ~30–40 fewer results.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eWhen to use:\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eWhen speed is the top priority and a small reduction in results is acceptable.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eDetailed:\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eReturns more results than Fast mode, but takes significantly longer.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eWhen to use:\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eBest for single-city or state level extraction where thoroughness matters.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eWhen not to use:\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eNot recommended for country-level extraction due to high time requirements.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eTip: Set \\\"Max Results\\\" to 1000 per city, otherwise large cities like New York can take 2+ hours.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eBy Zoom Level (15, 16, 17, 18):\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eVarious Zoom Level sizes are given below:\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eZoom 15: Neighborhood Level\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eZoom 16: Sub-Neighborhood Level\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eZoom 17: Block Level (Time consuming)\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eZoom 18: Street Level (Very time consuming)\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eWhen to use:\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eWhen you want the absolute highest results. Use Zoom 18. It can take 3-4 hours, but you will have thousands of results for big cities like New York City.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eWhen not to use:\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eNot recommended for country-level extraction. It can take 3-4 hours for a single city.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eBy Geolocation:\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eSearch a specific latitude/longitude or inside a polygon.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eWhen to use:\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eGetting results that are only in a specific area, but not outside it.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch2 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" for any category and location you choose — restaurants, dentists, gyms, agencies, and more. In a few clicks you get a clean, exportable list of businesses along with their contact details, so you can reach out and sell to them.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Founders use it to find their first customers; students use it to gather data for research projects.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">If you are a developer, this app has a <strong>built-in API</strong>, an <a href=\"https://www.npmjs.com/package/botasaurus-desktop-api\" rel=\"nofollow\">npm package</a>, and a\n<a href=\"https://pypi.org/project/botasaurus-api/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Python library</a>, so you can integrate it into your Python or Node.js scripts and get results in under 5 minutes. The API can also run on <a href=\"https://github.com/omkarcloud/google-maps-scraper/blob/master/server-deployment.md\">AWS and GCP VMs</a>.</p>\n\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">What data can I get from it?</h3><a id=\"user-content-what-data-can-i-get-from-it\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: What data can I get from it?\" href=\"#what-data-can-i-get-from-it\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">For every business, you get the major data points you need to sell:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Name, Main Category, and Address</li>\n<li>Phone number</li>\n<li>Website</li>\n<li>Email and social profiles (via enrichment)</li>\n<li>Rating and number of reviews</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\">That's just the start — the tool extracts <strong>50+ data points</strong> in total. See the full list in <a href=\"https://github.com/omkarcloud/google-maps-scraper/blob/master/advanced.md#are-there-any-usage-limits\">advanced.md (Are there any usage limits?)</a>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">On top of the raw data, <strong>business enrichment</strong> gets you sales-ready contacts: verified emails, decision-makers (CEO, Founder, etc.), and phone carrier info so you don't waste time on landlines.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">How to use it?</h3><a id=\"user-content-how-to-use-it\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: How to use it?\" href=\"#how-to-use-it\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">To use this tool, ensure you have <strong>Google Chrome</strong> installed. Ten minutes here and you will completely understand how to use the tool to get maximum leads.</p>\n\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a href=\"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1zCenEuKvjc\" rel=\"nofollow\"><img src=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/omkarcloud/google-maps-scraper/master/screenshots/video-thumbnails/detailed-demo.png\" alt=\"Detailed Demo (5 min) — Google Maps Scraper Full Tutorial\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">What is my auth token?</h3><a id=\"user-content-what-is-my-auth-token\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: What is my auth token?\" href=\"#what-is-my-auth-token\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Your auth token is <code>{{ AUTH_TOKEN }}</code>. You can use it when installing on an <a href=\"https://github.com/omkarcloud/google-maps-scraper/blob/master/server-deployment.md\">AWS EC2 server</a>.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">🔍 What are the various search strategies, and which one should I use?</h2><a id=\"user-content--what-are-the-various-search-strategies-and-which-one-should-i-use\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 🔍 What are the various search strategies, and which one should I use?\" href=\"#-what-are-the-various-search-strategies-and-which-one-should-i-use\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">This is slightly complex stuff, but if you want 10,000+ leads in a city, you must understand this.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">There are 5 kinds of search strategies:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Fast (Default)</li>\n<li>Fastest</li>\n<li>Detailed</li>\n<li>By Zoom Level (15, 16, 17, 18)</li>\n<li>By Geolocation</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/omkarcloud/google-maps-scraper/master/screenshots/search-strategies.png\"><img src=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/omkarcloud/google-maps-scraper/master/screenshots/search-strategies.png\" alt=\"search-strategies.png\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Let's see when to use which strategy:</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Fast (Default):</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Returns 120–1600 results per city.</li>\n<li>Completes in 1–10 minutes per city.</li>\n<li><strong>When to use:</strong>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Best for country-level extraction (completes in 1–2 days).</li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Fastest:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Slightly faster than Fast mode (~30 seconds per city), but returns ~30–40 fewer results.</li>\n<li><strong>When to use:</strong>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>When speed is the top priority and a small reduction in results is acceptable.</li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Detailed:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Returns more results than Fast mode, but takes significantly longer.</li>\n<li><strong>When to use:</strong>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Best for single-city or state level extraction where thoroughness matters.</li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n<li><strong>When not to use:</strong>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Not recommended for country-level extraction due to high time requirements.</li>\n<li>Tip: Set \"Max Results\" to 1000 per city, otherwise large cities like New York can take 2+ hours.</li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>By Zoom Level (15, 16, 17, 18):</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Various Zoom Level sizes are given below:\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Zoom 15: Neighborhood Level</li>\n<li>Zoom 16: Sub-Neighborhood Level</li>\n<li>Zoom 17: Block Level (Time consuming)</li>\n<li>Zoom 18: Street Level (Very time consuming)</li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n<li><strong>When to use:</strong>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>When you want the absolute highest results. Use Zoom 18. It can take 3-4 hours, but you will have thousands of results for big cities like New York City.</li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n<li><strong>When not to use:</strong>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Not recommended for country-level extraction. It can take 3-4 hours for a single city.</li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>By Geolocation:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Search a specific latitude/longitude or inside a polygon.</li>\n<li><strong>When to use:</strong>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Getting results that are only in a specific area, but not outside it.</li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">✨ Enrichment: Same Leads, Higher Conversion</h2><a id=\"user-content--enrichment-same-leads-higher-conversion\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: ✨ Enrichment: Same Leads, Higher Conversion\" href=\"#-enrichment-same-leads-higher-conversion\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Enrichment turns Google Maps business data into sales-ready contacts—same leads, same effort, but much higher conversion.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Phone:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Phone Carrier Info — know if it's a landline or mobile. Mobile numbers go to business owners and lead to better sales; landlines go to receptionists.</li>\n<li>Place Sales Summary — Personalize your pitch, so the customer sees that you have done your research and becomes interested in your product.</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Email:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Find the best email for outreach, with deliverability verification.</li>\n<li>Get social profiles, like Twitter, LinkedIn, etc., to get more information about the customer.</li>\n</ul>\n\n<p dir=\"auto\">Follow these steps to start using enrichment:</p>\n<ol dir=\"auto\">\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Sign up on Omkar Cloud by visiting <a href=\"https://www.omkar.cloud/auth/sign-up\" rel=\"nofollow\">this link</a>.</p>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Visit the <a href=\"https://www.omkar.cloud/api-key\" rel=\"nofollow\">API Key Page</a>.</p>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Copy the API key.\n<a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/omkarcloud/google-maps-scraper/master/screenshots/enrichment-key-omkar.png\"><img src=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/omkarcloud/google-maps-scraper/master/screenshots/enrichment-key-omkar.png\" alt=\"Copy the API Key\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Enter the copied API key in the \"Enrichment\" section:\n<a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/omkarcloud/google-maps-scraper/master/screenshots/enrichment-section.png\"><img src=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/omkarcloud/google-maps-scraper/master/screenshots/enrichment-section.png\" alt=\"enrichment-section.png\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Go to <a href=\"https://www.omkar.cloud/account/verify-phone\" rel=\"nofollow\">verify phone page</a> and verify your phone number. We require phone verification to prevent abuse of our free credits and keep the service available for genuine users.</p>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Run it.</p>\n</li>\n</ol>\n<p dir=\"auto\">That's it. You can find enrichment results in the \"Overview\" View.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/omkarcloud/google-maps-scraper/master/screenshots/enrichment-results.png\"><img src=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/omkarcloud/google-maps-scraper/master/screenshots/enrichment-results.png\" alt=\"enrichment-results.png\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">To get the most out of enrichment, read the <a href=\"https://www.omkar.cloud/tools/business-enrichment/about\" rel=\"nofollow\">Enrichment Documentation</a>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a href=\"https://www.omkar.cloud/tools/business-enrichment\" rel=\"nofollow\">Start enriching for free</a>—100 credits free/month. No credit card needed.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">💰 Pricing</h2><a id=\"user-content--pricing\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 💰 Pricing\" href=\"#-pricing\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><strong>Free — Free, 200 searches/month.</strong> Getting your first 20,000+ leads. No credit card needed.</li>\n<li><strong>Starter — $16/month, 5,000 searches/month.</strong> Getting clients every month, in city after city.</li>\n<li><strong>Unlimited — $48/month, Unlimited searches.</strong> Agencies and heavy users extracting country-level data at scale.</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Each search yields <strong>100 to 1,000+ results</strong> (10,000+ with the Detailed/Zoom 18 strategy). Apify and Outscraper charge you per result; we charge per search — so 5,000 searches can mean hundreds of thousands of leads. That's the cheapest per-lead cost in the market.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Start with <strong>Free</strong> — 200 searches/month is more than enough to test the tool and get up to 20,000+ leads per month. Upgrade to <strong>Starter ($16/month)</strong> when you're doing regular outreach and need more cities every month. Go <strong>Unlimited ($48/month)</strong> if you're extracting country-level data at scale.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Cancel anytime. Refund in 2 clicks, no questions asked:</strong></p>\n<ol dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Go to the <a href=\"https://www.omkar.cloud/billing/transaction-history\" rel=\"nofollow\">Transactions Page</a>.</li>\n<li>Click <strong>Request Refund</strong>, then confirm by clicking <strong>Request Refund</strong> again.</li>\n</ol>\n<p dir=\"auto\">That's it. The amount is refunded in 1-2 business days. No emails, no explanations, no questions asked.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Watch the full 2-click refund process:</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a href=\"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d5Xkwomrens\" rel=\"nofollow\"><img src=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/omkarcloud/google-maps-scraper/master/screenshots/video-thumbnails/refund.png\" alt=\"2-Click Refund Process of Omkar Cloud\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">We have made the refund process easier than cutting a cake 🎂. Why? Because we are confident in the value our product brings 😎.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">🤙 Still unsure?</h2><a id=\"user-content--still-unsure\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 🤙 Still unsure?\" href=\"#-still-unsure\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Have questions before buying?
